| **Phase A — Before lock (opposition required — not optional politeness):** While the operator is **exploring**, **brainstorming**, or has **not** issued a final command, agents and **Lumin** **shall oppose** weak, hidden-assumption, or destructive paths — **plainly**, **with evidence**, and **with at least one concrete alternative** (solution-focused). **Opposition is a feature, not disrespect.** **Yes-ma'am compliance** (agreeing without challenge) is **system failure** — it produces systems that help no one and harm the operator’s interests. When a **clear assumption** appears (unstated premise, conflated subsystem, missing evidence, reversible treated as final), **surface it immediately** — the operator **does not know what he does not know**; filling that gap is **mandatory**, not “helpful extra.” Format when opposing: **(1)** what assumes · **(2)** why it may be wrong · **(3)** proposed path(s) with tradeoffs · **(4)** what proof would settle it. Naked contrarianism without solutions is **also** failure. Use **Lens D**, **§2.12** when load-bearing, **Article III** for irreversible/high-risk — **before** lock. | NORTH_STAR_SSOT.md | Partially Enforced | Mechanically Verified | Mechanically Verified (Live) | upgraded |  | Evidence: `scripts/verify-runner-telemetry-g815.mjs` has active path (exported start/boot/register function); `scripts/verify-runner-telemetry-g815.mjs` covered by preflight scripts: ['scripts/verify-truth-lockdown.mjs']; `scripts/verify-runner-telemetry-g815.mjs:20` in `data`: * Fetches JSON data from a specified URL with an x-command-key header.; `scripts/verify-runner-telemetry-g815.mjs:23` in `data`: * @param {string} commandKey The command key for authentication. |

| **Navigation truth (analogy is law, not decoration).** Course from Hawaii to New York **one degree off**, never corrected → you do not land “slightly east of Manhattan”; you land in the **wrong region**. **One percent off**, maintained across distance, time, and decisions, is **catastrophic** — not minor, not “within tolerance.” | NORTH_STAR_SSOT.md | Mechanically Enforced | Mechanically Verified | Mechanically Verified (Live) | corrected (static vs live) |  | Evidence: `services/truth-enforcement-spine.js` loaded by `server-full-runtime.js:94` (`./services/truth-enforcement-spine.js`); `services/truth-enforcement-spine.js` has active path (exported start/boot/register function); `services/truth-enforcement-spine.js` covered by preflight scripts: ['scripts/verify-receipt-truth.mjs', 'scripts/verify-truth-lockdown.mjs', 'scripts/audit-truth-enforcement.mjs']; `services/truth-enforcement-spine.js:2`: * SYNOPSIS: Truth enforcement spine — single import for all truth gates in production. |
| **Epistemic compounding.** A **false assumption**, **failed verification**, **misleading receipt**, **orphan PASS**, **code before blueprint**, or **THINK/GUESS treated as KNOW** is never an isolated defect. The **next** blueprint step, build, scheduler run, dashboard, and operator report **inherits the false premise as input**. Each layer **multiplies** error. **Decisions based on something one percent off are themselves off — again and again.** | NORTH_STAR_SSOT.md | Mechanically Enforced | Mechanically Verified | Mechanically Verified (Live) | corrected (static vs live) |  | Evidence: `services/chair-prediction-score-scheduler.js` loaded by `startup/boot-domains.js:237` (`../services/chair-prediction-score-scheduler.js`); `services/chair-prediction-score-scheduler.js` has active path (scheduled interval/timeout; exported start/boot/register function); `services/chair-prediction-score-scheduler.js` covered by preflight scripts: ['scripts/audit-false-done-steps.mjs']; `services/chair-prediction-score-scheduler.js:30` in `isDue`: function isDue(receipt) { |

| **Where load-bearing technical decisions are made.** A **load-bearing technical decision** includes (non-exhaustive): architecture, security and secrets handling, data model, API contracts, integration and automation strategy, choice among implementation options, and any fork that is hard to reverse or that affects money, health, safety, or compliance. Such decisions **must not** be resolved by a **single** model or a single session acting alone. The **AI Council** (multi-model routing and debate, per `docs/products/ai-council/PRODUCT_HOME.md` and `docs/SSOT_COMPANION.md` §5.5 / gate-change **consensus** protocol) **must** be used **after** the proposer has **reviewed** **authoritative external input where applicable** — including **industry and vendor best practices, security baselines, and current documentation** (which may require **responsible** web or vendor-doc research when facts are not in the repo). Unlabeled *THINK* or *GUESS* passed off as *KNOW* is a **§2.6** violation. | NORTH_STAR_SSOT.md | scripts/ssot-check.js, scripts/verify-product-home.mjs, services/council-service.js, services/ai-council-service.js, config/council-members.js | @ssot tag + product-home verification | Live | Mechanically Verified | Evidence: `scripts/ssot-check.js` covered by preflight scripts: ['scripts/ssot-check.js']; `scripts/ssot-check.js:32`: loadProductManifests,; `scripts/ssot-check.js:146` in `isCoCommitSourceFile`: if (file.includes('docs/')) return false;; `scripts/verify-product-home.mjs:14`: loadProductManifests, |

| **Phase A — Before lock (opposition required — not optional politeness):** While the operator is **exploring**, **brainstorming**, or has **not** issued a final command, agents and **Lumin** **shall oppose** weak, hidden-assumption, or destructive paths — **plainly**, **with evidence**, and **with at least one concrete alternative** (solution-focused). **Opposition is a feature, not disrespect.** **Yes-ma'am compliance** (agreeing without challenge) is **system failure** — it produces systems that help no one and harm the operator’s interests. When a **clear assumption** appears (unstated premise, conflated subsystem, missing evidence, reversible treated as final), **surface it immediately** — the operator **does not know what he does not know**; filling that gap is **mandatory**, not “helpful extra.” Format when opposing: **(1)** what assumes · **(2)** why it may be wrong · **(3)** proposed path(s) with tradeoffs · **(4)** what proof would settle it. Naked contrarianism without solutions is **also** failure. Use **Lens D**, **§2.12** when load-bearing, **Article III** for irreversible/high-risk — **before** lock. | NORTH_STAR_SSOT.md | scripts/verify-runner-telemetry-g815.mjs, routes/audit-intake-flow-routes.js, services/receipt-truth-validator.js, scripts/verify-mission-doctrine.mjs, services/lifeos-service-doctrine.js | blueprint/guardrail verification script | Live | Mechanically Verified | Evidence: `scripts/verify-runner-telemetry-g815.mjs` has active path (exported start/boot/register function); `scripts/verify-runner-telemetry-g815.mjs` covered by preflight scripts: ['scripts/verify-truth-lockdown.mjs']; `scripts/verify-runner-telemetry-g815.mjs:20` in `data`: * Fetches JSON data from a specified URL with an x-command-key header.; `scripts/verify-runner-telemetry-g815.mjs:23` in `data`: * @param {string} commandKey The command key for authentication. |

## 5. Authority Drift Report

| Domain | Competing Authorities | Drift Risk | Type |
|---|---|---|---|
| Constitutional supremacy | docs/constitution/NORTH_STAR_SSOT.md, docs/constitution/POINT_B_DNA.md, docs/constitution/UNIFIED_DOCTRINE_MAP.md, docs/constitution/COGNITIVE_CORE_LAWS.md, docs/constitution/LUMIN_COMMUNICATION_DNA.md, docs/constitution/LUMIN_DISPLAY_DNA.md, docs/constitution/FOUNDER_PACKET_V3_BUILDEROS_MASTER_ARCHITECTURE.md | Multiple documents assert supreme authority; no runtime tie-breaker exists. | Current Remediation |
| Work queue authority | builderos-reboot/BP_PRIORITY.json, builderos-reboot/MISSION_QUEUE.json, builderos-reboot/MISSION_PACK_INDEX.json | BP_PRIORITY claims canonical, but MISSION_QUEUE and MISSION_PACK_INDEX still exist and legacy autopilot references them. | Current Remediation |
| Product registry authority | docs/products/PRODUCT_REGISTRY.json, docs/products/INDEX.md | Two product indexes; unclear which is canonical for autonomous builders. | Current Remediation |
| Runtime authority | services/, factory-staging/, routes/ | `routes/` is legacy production spine; `factory-staging/` is canonical factory; both can ship code. | Current Remediation |
| Agent instruction authority | CLAUDE.md, docs/AGENT_RULES.compact.md, docs/AGENT_INBOX.md, .cursor/rules/system-ship-via-builder.mdc, .cursor/rules/legacy-interfaces-forbidden.mdc | Multiple rule files; `.cursor/rules` are local IDE rules, `CLAUDE.md` is general, `AGENT_RULES.compact` is runtime reference. | Future Prevention |
| SSOT authority | docs/SSOT_COMPANION.md, docs/constitution/*_SSOT*.md, docs/products/*/PRODUCT_HOME.md | SSOT documents and product homes both claim ownership of product truth. | Future Prevention |

## 6. File Creation Governance Review

### 6.1 Proposed Level Model
- **Level 1 — Ordinary implementation**: new `services/`, `routes/`, `core/` files for a ratified product.
- **Level 2 — Architectural authority**: new `startup/` registrations, `factory-staging/` canonical builders, runtime profile gates.
- **Level 3 — Constitutional authority**: changes to `docs/constitution/`, `CLAUDE.md`, `.cursor/rules/*.mdc`, `builderos-reboot/governance/`, and any new canonical index/registry.

### 6.2 Stress-test / loopholes
- **Misclassification**: an agent can mark a Level 3 change as Level 1 by placing authority language inside an ordinary `services/` helper. Without a lint/parser checking for authority keywords, the label is self-reported.
- **Self-approval**: a builder commit can include both the new file and a `FILE_CREATION_DECISION` receipt authored by the same commit, so "review" is not independent.
- **Path loophole**: `.cursor/rules/*.mdc` and `CLAUDE.md` are not covered by `system:commit-files` receipts unless the gate explicitly enumerates them.
- **Historical drift**: existing competing authorities were created before any gate, so a forward-only gate does not remediate current drift.
- **Bypass via direct Railway shell**: anyone with Railway access can create files and deploy without a git commit, so the gate is enforceable only at the commit/deploy boundary it controls.

### 6.3 Recommended objective escalation triggers
A file creation gate should auto-escalate when any of the following are true:
- File lives in `docs/constitution/`, `builderos-reboot/governance/`, `.cursor/rules/`, `startup/`, or root-level `CLAUDE.md`.
- File path/name contains `registry`, `index`, `queue`, `priority`, `ssot`, `constitution`, `doctrine`, `governance`, `authority`, `canonical`.
- File exports `register*`, `start*Scheduler`, `boot*`, or defines an Express router/app.
- File writes to or creates a new JSON index/queue/manifest.
- File declares `@authority` or `@ssot` to a document it did not also update.
- Pre-commit diff adds a new directory under `docs/products/` without an updated `PRODUCT_REGISTRY.json` and `FILE_MANIFEST.json`.

## 7. Mechanical Enforcement Plan for File Creation Gate

### 7.1 Goal
No protected file can be committed or deployed without a `FILE_CREATION_DECISION` receipt that records the level, author, rationale, reviewer, and evidence.

### 7.2 Receipt format
Append to `builderos-reboot/governance/FILE_CREATION_DECISIONS.jsonl`:
```jsonl
{"ts":"2026-07-30T...","path":"services/new-service.js","level":1,"product":"site-builder","author":"devin","rationale":"SMOS video export helper","reviewed_by":"council","evidence":"BP_PRIORITY.json mission id or SENTRY receipt","sha":"<commit-sha>"}
```

### 7.3 Enforcement chain (not implemented here)
1. **Pre-commit hook** `scripts/file-creation-gate.mjs`:
   - Diff staged files against `builderos-reboot/governance/FILE_CREATION_DECISIONS.jsonl`.
   - If a new protected file lacks a receipt, exit 1 with a concrete message.
2. **System ship enforcement**: `system:commit-files.mjs` and `POST /api/v1/lifeos/builder/execute-batch` call the gate before `commitToGitHub`.
3. **Runtime boot loader**: `server.js` / `startup/boot-domains.js` rejects loading a module whose receipt `sha` does not match the current checkout or is missing for protected paths.
4. **Preflight verification**: `node scripts/file-creation-gate.mjs --verify` runs in `builder:preflight`.

### 7.4 Sufficiency and limits
- Sufficient to prevent *git-tracked* future authority drift if enforced at commit and boot.
- **Documentation Only** until the hook, ship path, and boot loader are wired and tested.
- Does not remediate existing competing authorities; Mission 2 must archive/supersede those.
- Railway shell / direct-upload can still bypass git, so the gate is only as strong as the deploy boundary it guards.

## 8. Calibration Example: §2.0G Governance Evolution Law

- **Runtime component**: `services/governance-review-scheduler.js`
- **Load evidence**: imported at `server-founder-runtime.js:60` and invoked at `server-founder-runtime.js:547`.
- **Execution stage**: `Executing` — the scheduler is started inside `bootFounderRuntime()`.
- **Verification barrier**: `Verification Blocked — Tooling Gap` — we cannot independently prove the scheduled function fired on Railway; only code inspection and the explicit commit comment confirm the wiring.
- **Corrected classification**: `Mechanically Enforced (Live)` with a `THINK` caveat on continuous verification.

## 9. Existing Drift vs Future Prevention

| Finding | Category | Action |
|---|---|---|
| Multiple constitutional documents all claim supremacy. | Current Remediation | Establish a single canonical `NORTH_STAR_SSOT.md` and explicitly subordinate other docs by amendment/version. |
| `BP_PRIORITY.json`, `MISSION_QUEUE.json`, `MISSION_PACK_INDEX.json` co-exist. | Current Remediation | Archive `MISSION_QUEUE.json` and `MISSION_PACK_INDEX.json` to `HIST` or delete after confirming no active code path uses them as queue authority. |
| `docs/products/PRODUCT_REGISTRY.json` vs `docs/products/INDEX.md`. | Current Remediation | Pick one registry and redirect the other to it; update `builder:preflight` to assert single source. |
| `routes/` (legacy production spine) vs `factory-staging/` (canonical factory). | Current Remediation | Document cutover receipts and stop adding new canonical builder logic to `routes/`. |
| `.cursor/rules/*.mdc`, `CLAUDE.md`, `AGENT_RULES.compact.md` provide overlapping instructions. | Future Prevention | File Creation Gate Level 3 must require explicit review and `FILE_CREATION_DECISION` receipt for any agent-instruction file. |
| No `FILE_CREATION_DECISION` receipt or mechanical gate exists today. | Future Prevention | Implement `scripts/file-creation-gate.mjs` and enforce at commit, ship, and boot. |
